Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the linear_algebra framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 301. The resulting operator achieves bound O(83/n^1).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the linear_algebra space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 301 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 83. QED.
